## [Codex | 2026-09-20] Usage efficiency without reduced quality
User reports high weekly Pro usage and asks to reduce overhead while preserving all approved depth/quality. Explicitly does not want a weaker model; retain Astra/current chosen reasoning. No scope reduction authorized.
Workflow going forward: group related implementation into coherent playable milestones rather than deploying every small addition; retain small local commits. Reuse tested quest/checkpoint/co-op/voice systems while keeping authored environments and mechanics unique. Keep a compact current-task code/test map with links to full canonical requirements, preserving original sources. Use narrow reads and concise pass/fail outputs; inspect full logs on failure. Run focused checks during edits and appropriate broader/save/co-op/visual checks at release boundaries; repeat only for relevant changes or unresolved risks. Batch release documentation and archives at milestone boundaries. Avoid a large refactor solely for hoped-for savings. No guaranteed savings percentage, no model/settings/plugin changes, and no claim that later levels will be cheap. Next milestone remains Brute encounter, followed by Rift Hall.

## [Codex | 2026-09-21] Voice Studio stereo recording — user request

User reports playback sounding mono and asks for stereo. Studio now requests two microphone channels and records through an explicit two-channel Web Audio destination at 192 kbps. Native stereo is preserved; mono sources are copied to both ears. A Centered voice option downmixes then duplicates for interfaces carrying a microphone on one side. No synthetic stereo widening, no rewrite of existing recordings or approvals. Input/output explanation is shown beside the recorder. The browser's native audio element already centers ordinary mono playback. Validated real MediaRecorder files by decoding: mono-to-stereo has two matching non-silent channels; stereo retains distinct channels; centered two-channel input produces matching channels. Microphone access remains user initiated. New module and cache-busted studio load independently of the pending Frostfell boss release.

## [Codex | 2026-09-22] Great Furnace and Colossus implemented — 2.014

U167 continues the approved campaign queue. The Colossus is six player-heights tall, with a raised horseshoe, ground-accessible cooling valves, optional jumping shortcuts and a safe lower floor. Turn a valve for a 13-second damage window; tanks refill over 18 seconds. Locked slam circles, marked vents and a low sweep share warning/hit geometry. Upper shelves avoid the sweep. One two-person repair crew appears below 55% health; repairs cap at 8% maximum HP. No legacy crystal-shield phase. ED-05 is hidden behind a cooled rear pipe after victory and banks immediately. The overhead camera widens for this encounter; close camera modes remain player-controlled. Enemy/faction identifiers preserved.

## [Codex | 2026-10-03] Endless roguelite and enclosed cameras
Approved direction: preserve Abyssal Descent as campaign grinding. Build roguelite on Endless Dungeons, separate progression; retain all earned gold across death and spend between attempts. Buy classes directly or qualify through dungeon achievements/quests before purchase. User wants enclosed ceilings, no seeing over walls into adjacent rooms, richer procedural topology/verticality, required parkour, varied waves/encounters/minibosses/bosses, and co-op. Current request includes fixing first-person legacy hand/world rendering and making shoulder/overhead cameras handle enclosed spaces.
Grounded audit: loadEndlessArena currently builds one 940-unit square; this needs a new connected room generator, not decoration alone. Immediate camera fix: queue the actual 3D hero/world in FPS, suppress legacy viewmodel when ready, temporarily hide local head in FPS; ray-sweep third-person camera against solid architecture and explicit future ceiling volumes. Preserve platform opacity and restore head for other views/peers. Document the larger generator and co-op plan separately; do not claim roguelite implemented.


## [Codex | 2026-10-03] Approved: health attrition defines dungeon runs
User approves the dungeon plan and makes HP management central over a long run. Early equipment offers limited protection; mistakes take meaningful chunks of health and healing opportunities are scarce. Better armor improves how many hits can be survived, while better weapons reduce exposure by killing enemies faster. A skilled fresh character must still be able to progress substantially through avoiding attacks, rather than encountering mandatory upgrade checks.
Implementation implications: carry health between floors; replace/rebalance the current automatic 20-percent floor heal rather than retaining it by accident. Healing frequency, damage amounts and upgrade strength remain playtest-tuned, not locked numeric values. Preserve readable attack warnings and escape windows; avoid unavoidable damage and spawn-on-player hits. Audit healing/lifesteal builds and co-op recovery so they do not trivially erase attrition, without silently removing class identities. These implications guide the proposed rework; no combat/economy changes implemented in this documentation update.


## [Codex | 2026-10-03] Approved direction: roguelite endpoint and harder progression
User requires an endpoint for the roguelite and suggests NG+ or graduation to a harder, different tier afterward. This applies only to the dungeon roguelite; campaign NG+ remains removed. Exact floor count, tier names/count, scaling and unlock details are not yet locked.
Proposed design for review: a finite expedition through multiple regions ending in a final boss and victory/results screen. Winning unlocks a selectable higher difficulty tier without removing access to the earlier tier. Retain earned gold, purchased classes/upgrades and achievement/quest progress; begin each attempt with its normal fresh run equipment/temporary-power reset. Higher tiers change encounter compositions, room variants, hazards and boss mechanics, alongside restrained numeric scaling. Preserve avoidable damage and viable fresh-build skill expression; do not make progression an unlimited enemy-HP treadmill. Victory should be at least as valid a banking endpoint as death or voluntary checkpoint exit. This is a plan only, not an implemented mode change.

Gold persists; upgrades bought between runs. Host can resume own checkpoint solo/co-op; guests retain their own solo checkpoint. Increased co-op enemy health (~1.6x for two as initial tuning), no automatic damage doubling.
Implementation correction: actual Endless Dungeon is G.delve/startDelve and already uses campaign escrow and branching rooms. G.endless/startEndless is Abyssal Descent and must remain unchanged. Earlier notes attributing the boxed arena to the target mode were wrong. Build new prison module and integrate only the Delve entry/runtime; use protected per-character mode profile and stable room reward receipts. Exact class offers and numeric costs are initial tunable content, not story canon.

World: retain the sense of scale of current main campaign levels while filling empty traversal meaningfully. Rolling terrain and hills should shape sightlines and fights, not merely platforms on a flat plane. Props must look appropriate, have intentional placement and correct collisions. Environmentally justified spawns/ambushes; alternate pressure and recovery without enemy spam or long cleared dash-only stretches. Preserve mill clarity: uncomplicated observable logic, visible environmental consequences and working presentation.

## [Codex | 2026-10-07] Endless Dungeon revision-8 side route

Implementation choice within the authorized quality pass: add a genuinely optional traversal challenge in each section rather than another encounter reskin. Retain host-authoritative co-op rewards and the current main-route and chest progression. Browser physics verifies reachability; human playtesting remains necessary for enjoyment and challenge.

After inspecting the shipped cache room in a normal camera, its square plinth supports still read as crude blocks. Replace art-only support meshes with narrow tapered stone piers, restrained iron collars and section-specific stepping surfaces; keep physical colliders and revision-8 plans fixed. The change raises art detail within the existing draw-call budget but does not claim to settle the final aesthetic.

The user requested a full bestiary by the Sparring Room with model-face portraits, encounter locations and stats. The complete roster is visible immediately because discovery gating was not requested. Baseline numbers are explicitly distinguished from scaled in-run values. No save-schema change or SFX work.

The user's broad hub improvement request authorizes a cohesive quality pass on the existing Lantern Court. ## [Codex | 2026-10-07] Larger roguelite and colorful art direction

User-approved: expand the Endless Dungeon/Prison Break substantially and incorporate main-campaign enemies. This supersedes the earlier original-only enemy constraint for this mode. Implementation must version fresh layouts and preserve saved runs.

## [Codex | 2026-10-07] Endless Dungeon objective variety

Further improvement request follows the 16-room expansion. Grounded finding: the new workroom and yard still shared the ordinary clear-all loop. For revision-10 fresh runs, the workroom has two breakable wards that call capped reinforcements until destroyed; the yard charges while either living co-op player holds a visible center circle, with groups arriving at charge milestones. The former creates target priority; the latter creates positional pressure and a pause/recovery decision. Old runs retain their previous rules and geometry. No SFX work.

## [Codex | 2026-10-07] Normal-speed Prison Break pacing | U-2026-10-07-PRISON-PACING

Oliver authorized the proposed normal-speed playthrough and fixes. Browser observation: walking through the first guard room left newly spawned enemies behind and pointed the objective backward. Revision-11 fresh attempts therefore spawn required guards on approach and visibly seal each required room's outgoing corridor until that room clears. This is a route-pacing correction, not a new enemy or reward rule. Revisions 1-10, optional paths, save slot economy and co-op host authority remain unchanged. Automated/browser checks establish the implementation, while human combat feel remains open. See docs/design/ENDLESS_DUNGEON_PACING_2026-10-07.md.

## [Codex | 2026-10-08] Full enemy appearance and motion upgrade | U-2026-10-08-ENEMY-ART

Oliver requested the complete enemy roster appearance and animation pass. The earlier 41-model pass was a first art pass; this release rebuilds those 41 and adds seven previously block-rendered Prison Break foes. All standard types get a deliberate skin palette and signature detail, and all 50 skinned types (including the two bespoke Colossi) get anatomy-aware idle, gait, attack, hit and death motion. Boss moves with distinct mechanics receive separate animation variants; the Hydra remains procedural but its bite/head movement is smoothed. Keep gameplay authority, collision, rewards and saves unchanged. SFX remains reserved for Oliver. Browser and save checks passed; subjective art and feel remain for player review.

## [Codex | 2026-10-08] Further enemy roster refinement | U-2026-10-08-ENEMY-ART-FOLLOWUP

It improves the shared movement clips and pairs every named boss strike with its own recovery. Maintain the current cast, collision and combat timing. The render and browser audits establish coverage and technical stability, while final naturalness and art taste remain player judgments. No SFX work.

Oliver prefers a thorough quality pass on the existing full-scale campaign levels rather than replacing them all with compact beta layouts. ## [Codex | 2026-10-18] Campaign boss tactics and difficulty

User: "Right now the bosses are all too easy. And I think we should in some way, make the environmental strategies partly necessary to defeat them. Marksman is way too easy". Approved scope: campaign boss difficulty/mechanic pass, prioritizing Marksman. Preserve earned progression, distinct encounters, existing layouts/story and cooperative host authority; no trial/PvP rebalance.

Inspection: Marksman has no approach defense and can lose the entire first perch to burst before its55% relocation check runs. Brute/cart and Fallen/pillar stagger offer little practical advantage over direct damage. Furnace has only12% hot-armor damage and13-second exposure; Marble exposes for12 seconds. Hydra already requires three restraint windows. Officers have no actual protective shield relationship. statusDamage directly subtracts HP, bypassing all normal armor/exposure protections. Host guest-execute handling fails to exclude bosses. Those bypasses undermine the authored strategies independently of HP tuning.

Plan: one campaign-only damage policy shared by direct hits and status damage, with real vulnerability windows and encounter-specific bounds: Brute cart stagger; Marksman defended perches, guaranteed relocation and covered-shot/flank opening; Fallen recovery and pillar stagger; Officers shield-facing/proximity protection so separating/flanking matters; Furnace cooling windows; Hydra existing three restraint openings; Marble reflected-screen openings; King's recovery/add phases. Keep cover/climb alternatives and reachable ground paths, clear short hints/status text, no generic combination puzzle. Moderate fixed regional boss HP increase, no scaling to the player's current gear. Fix guest height and boss execution authority. Validate huge bursts, status/pet/guest paths, all mechanics, co-op snapshots, normal starter combat and old saves in muted browser; numerical checks do not establish novice fun or subjective difficulty.

## [Codex | 2026-10-18] Endless Dungeon recovery and campaign assets · v2.306.0

Older runs retain their prior roster/layout.

Reused existing cached campaign furniture, supplies, barricades, cell fronts and sconces, merged by material and fitted to existing cover footprints. Refectory tables use plain bench construction, not forge workbenches/anvils. Collision-only cell volumes no longer draw as opaque walls over the bars. Floor enemy assets prefetch behind the loading curtain. All dungeon sale paths now use receipt-backed dungeon credit and checkpoint the sale/removal; resume retains sale gold and sold gear stays removed, duplicate receipt yields0. Campaign sales retain original behavior.
